# DATA archive, 2026-10

Entries moved out of DATA.md by scripts/archive_logs.py to keep the living
file small enough to read cheaply. Nothing here is edited or summarised: it is
the original text, newest first. DATA.md links back to this file.

- Measuring since 2026-08-30, when Measure.swift went in. Unlinked to any account by design: an install id, the app version and the OS, nothing else.
- 85 installs have ever sent anything. An install id is made fresh on each install, so this is an upper bound on people, not a count of them.
- Yesterday came from 3 installs, and the busiest made 22 of 3 events (733%). One install making most of a day is what our own reinstall looks like: add its id to excluded_installs in data/app-measure.json if it is ours.
- Tabs opened (7d): Discover 10; My trees 8; Map 6
- Active phones: 3 in the last day, 24 in the last 7 days, 56 in the last 30 days.
- Coming back: 26 of those 56 phones opened the app on more than one day (46%).
- Trees opened by the most phones (7d): The Fig of the Glorieta de Goya (Seville) 2; The Great White Oak of Logan (Logan Oak) (Logan, Ohio) 2; The Ombú of La Cartuja (Seville) 1; The Mulberry of the Alamillo Cortijo (Seville) 1; The Magnolia of the Cathedral (Seville) 1
- Where the phones are (30d): United States 13; United Kingdom 7; Portugal 5; Spain 4; Italy 4; The Netherlands 4
- Trees recorded (7d): a tree we map 1
  A sighting reaches our database only through an account. Signed out it stays on the phone, so this count can run ahead of the trees and photographs we actually receive.

**Where demand is going to waste**

Search Console, the last 10 days Google will give us (its data lags 2-3 days, so the newest row is never yesterday). The newest row is also still FILLING: Google backfills for days and backfills impressions harder than clicks, so it reads as an unusually good day and is not one (08-22 arrived as 12/769 and settled at 17/1030). Position is an average across every query, so it dips whenever we start ranking for something new:

**Where demand is going to waste**

Search Console, the last 10 days Google will give us (its data lags 2-3 days, so the newest row is never yesterday). **Copy test**

```
Copy test: city-title-age-first (day 34 of 56, review 2026-11-05)
The age is the hook and the count is inventory, so naming the oldest tree before the number earns more clicks.

  control      n1    before 1.38  now 0.00  change -1.38

  TOO EARLY. At about 2 clicks per page per ten days this needs the full window; a lead now is noise wearing a result's clothes.
```

- Ours is not in this table: 22 install(s), 961 events, the testing before the app went live on 2026-09-03 and every install built by Xcode since (the app says how it was built with each event). An App Store copy on our own phone is the one kind this cannot see.

- Yesterday came from 3 installs, and the busiest made 12 of 3 events (400%). One install making most of a day is what our own reinstall looks like: add its id to excluded_installs in data/app-measure.json if it is ours.

**Where demand is going to waste**

Search Console, the last 10 days Google will give us (its data lags 2-3 days, so the newest row is never yesterday). The newest row is also still FILLING: Google backfills for days and backfills impressions harder than clicks, so it reads as an unusually good day and is not one (08-22 arrived as 12/769 and settled at 17/1030). Position is an average across every query, so it dips whenever we start ranking for something new:

```
Copy test: city-title-age-first (day 33 of 56, review 2026-11-05)

  control      n1    before 1.38  now 0.00  change -1.38

  TOO EARLY. At about 2 clicks per page per ten days this needs the full window; a lead now is noise wearing a result's clothes.
```

## Weekly analysis 2026-10-12

Eleventh weekly analysis. Compares 10-05 to 10-11 with 09-28 to 10-04, read
from the daily entries. 10-11 is partial in Search Console. The prior row is
the figure written last week, so Google's later backfill can move it a little.

**Scorecard**

| Week | Impressions | Clicks | CTR | Avg position | Visits (beacon) | Directions (14d) | App-CTA (14d) | App downloads (first-time) | Waitlist total | Submissions total |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 09-28 to 10-04 | 400 | 14 | 3.5% | 18 to 39 | 220 (8d) | 34 | 22 | 13 | 20 | 32 |
| 10-05 to 10-11 | 277 | 14 | 5.1% | 22 to 50 | 330 (7d) | 5 | 19 | 14 (3 days absent from the table) | 21 | 33 |

Device split (beacon, 7d): mobile 600 pageviews, desktop 270. Search clicks over
28 days: mobile 523 of 727.

**What moved:**
1. Google is still at the floor and slowly lower: 277 impressions in 7 days
   (about 40 a day, from about 57), 14 clicks, position 22 to 50. No
   recovery signal. The CTR rise is 14 clicks; it means nothing.
2. Bing now shows us more than Google does: 537 impressions and 18 clicks
   for 10-04 to 10-10, against 47 and 4 the week before, with the index at
   2,157 pages and no demotion. Its 18 clicks are more than Google's 14.
3. The one page with real exposure converts nothing. Prague holds 325 of
   the 462 impressions in the 10-day window at position 5.6 with 0 clicks;
   seolearn puts the loss at about 19 clicks per 10 days. Its pages
   (/prague/walks plus tree pages) climbed 18 to 33 impressions each.
4. Site visits 330 (7 days), 62% of pageviews moved between our own pages,
   and Google sent 20 visits. Directions fell from 34 to 5 on the 14-day
   count, the clearest product fall, but it follows the visit drop and the
   counts are small.
5. The app is steady: 14 first-time downloads (10 of the last 21 from App
   Store search), 13 new phones, 2 collected a tree on day one (15%, 33% the
   week before), back-within-a-week 56%. 5 accounts opened, 1 Android
   waitlist signup, 1 reader tree sent. The machine produced nothing in the
   last 24 hours (4 knocks, 0 minutes, 0 trees); the week meter reads 791
   minutes.

**Suggestions, ranked:**
1. **[run]** Fix the Prague title and meta for the query "prague" (i15 at
   positions 1 to 9, 0 clicks, about 19 clicks lost per 10 days). It is the
   only page where position is good and clicks are zero, so it looks like a
   snippet problem, not a content problem. First check whether those
   impressions are rank trackers rather than readers; if so, drop Prague
   from the depth list.
2. **[run]** Treat Bing as a real channel: pull its queries and pages for
   10-04 to 10-10 (queries such as "remarkable trees copenhagen" and "oldest
   tree in belfast" already click at positions 1 to 6) and give those pages
   the photograph, register link and access line first. Bing pages are
   earning clicks now when Google pages are not.
3. **[run]** Keep the sighting queue as rung 1 every knock. Only tree pages
   with a photograph are indexed, and reader photographs are the one steady
   source of new ones (a tree collected in the app in Palma on 10-11, Seville
   on 10-08).
4. **[sessie]** Find out why the machine did nothing on 4 knocks in a row
   (usage window closed, or the knock failing). Enrichment is the only work
   recovery mode allows and idle time is the largest unused lever.
5. **[FOR HIDDE]** The recovery-mode question stands and has gained a week:
   still about 40 impressions a day after 14 days. The Search Console Manual
   actions and Page indexing checks from last week are not answered in the
   data, and they decide between waiting and a reconsideration request.

**Watch next week:** Google daily impressions above 100, whether the Prague
CTR moves off 0%, Bing staying above Google, and whether the machine produces
trees again.

- Trees recorded (14d): a tree we map 18; a tree we do not 4

**Where demand is going to waste**

Search Console, the last 10 days Google will give us (its data lags 2-3 days, so the newest row is never yesterday). The newest row is also still FILLING: Google backfills for days and backfills impressions harder than clicks, so it reads as an unusually good day and is not one (08-22 arrived as 12/769 and settled at 17/1030). Position is an average across every query, so it dips whenever we start ranking for something new:

**What converts, and what does not** (scripts/seolearn.py)

```
SEO learning pass: 1 pages over 40 impressions, 1 of them clean
Index 1.00 means a page converts exactly as its position normally does.

SHARE OF TREES CARRYING A PHOTOGRAPH
   40% and over           n1   i325   c0    ctr  0.0%  index 0.00

SHARE WITH A CONFIRMED PIN
   40% and over           n1   i325   c0    ctr  0.0%  index 0.00

SHARE WITH A RECOGNITION LINE
   40% and over           n1   i325   c0    ctr  0.0%  index 0.00

SHARE WITH A RECORDED AGE
   40% and over           n1   i325   c0    ctr  0.0%  index 0.00

SHARE WITH A SEASONAL PEAK
   40% and over           n1   i325   c0    ctr  0.0%  index 0.00

TREES ON THE PAGE
   over 25                n1   i325   c0    ctr  0.0%  index 0.00

AVERAGE STORY LENGTH
   170 and over           n1   i325   c0    ctr  0.0%  index 0.00

EARNED AND WASTED, worst first (real queries, converting under half)
These have the demand already. Fixing one is worth more than a new page.
   prague           i325   c0   p5.6   index 0.00  loses ~19 clicks/10d  -
```

**Copy test**

```
Copy test: city-title-age-first (day 32 of 56, review 2026-11-05)
The age is the hook and the count is inventory, so naming the oldest tree before the number earns more clicks.

  control      n1    before 1.38  now 0.00  change -1.38

  TOO EARLY. At about 2 clicks per page per ten days this needs the full window; a lead now is noise wearing a result's clothes.
```

**Where demand is going to waste**

Search Console, the last 10 days Google will give us (its data lags 2-3 days, so the newest row is never yesterday). The newest row is also still FILLING: Google backfills for days and backfills impressions harder than clicks, so it reads as an unusually good day and is not one (08-22 arrived as 12/769 and settled at 17/1030). Position is an average across every query, so it dips whenever we start ranking for something new:

- Top pages (10d): / (c4/i11); /brussels/oldest-tree (c3/i4); /amsterdam/oudemanhuispoort-elm (c1/i1); /assisi (c1/i1); /brisbane/white-rabbit-tree-of-newstead-park (c1/i1)
- Content leads, biggest first. A lead is a query no page TITLE covers, so some are ranking problems on a page we have rather than a page to write ('vegas trees' against /las-vegas). Check before building:

```
TREES ON THE PAGE
   over 25                n1   i307   c0    ctr  0.0%  index 0.00

AVERAGE STORY LENGTH
   170 and over           n1   i307   c0    ctr  0.0%  index 0.00

EARNED AND WASTED, worst first (real queries, converting under half)
These have the demand already. Fixing one is worth more than a new page.
   prague           i307   c0   p5.5   index 0.00  loses ~18 clicks/10d  -
```

**Copy test**

```
Copy test: city-title-age-first (day 31 of 56, review 2026-11-05)
The age is the hook and the count is inventory, so naming the oldest tree before the number earns more clicks.

  control      n1    before 1.38  now 0.00  change -1.38

  TOO EARLY. At about 2 clicks per page per ten days this needs the full window; a lead now is noise wearing a result's clothes.
```

- Near and location count only phones on a version that sends them (from 2026-10-10); a dash means none yet.
- Back within a week looks at the phones that arrived a week earlier, so each has had its seven days.

**Where demand is going to waste**

Search Console, the last 10 days Google will give us (its data lags 2-3 days, so the newest row is never yesterday). The newest row is also still FILLING: Google backfills for days and backfills impressions harder than clicks, so it reads as an unusually good day and is not one (08-22 arrived as 12/769 and settled at 17/1030). Position is an average across every query, so it dips whenever we start ranking for something new:

**Copy test**

```
Copy test: city-title-age-first (day 30 of 56, review 2026-11-05)
The age is the hook and the count is inventory, so naming the oldest tree before the number earns more clicks.

  control      n1    before 1.38  now 0.05  change -1.32

  TOO EARLY. At about 2 clicks per page per ten days this needs the full window; a lead now is noise wearing a result's clothes.
```

**Conclusion:** Search is the only channel with real data and it is still small: 1 clicks and 9 impressions on Google's freshest day (day before: c0/i47). Directional at best; no strategic conclusions from these volumes.

**What converts, and what does not** (scripts/seolearn.py)

```
SEO learning pass: 1 pages over 40 impressions, 1 of them clean
Index 1.00 means a page converts exactly as its position normally does.

SHARE OF TREES CARRYING A PHOTOGRAPH
   40% and over           n1   i300   c1    ctr  0.3%  index 0.05

SHARE WITH A CONFIRMED PIN
   40% and over           n1   i300   c1    ctr  0.3%  index 0.05

SHARE WITH A RECOGNITION LINE
   40% and over           n1   i300   c1    ctr  0.3%  index 0.05

SHARE WITH A RECORDED AGE
   40% and over           n1   i300   c1    ctr  0.3%  index 0.05

SHARE WITH A SEASONAL PEAK
   40% and over           n1   i300   c1    ctr  0.3%  index 0.05

TREES ON THE PAGE
   over 25                n1   i300   c1    ctr  0.3%  index 0.05

AVERAGE STORY LENGTH
   170 and over           n1   i300   c1    ctr  0.3%  index 0.05

EARNED AND WASTED, worst first (real queries, converting under half)
These have the demand already. Fixing one is worth more than a new page.
   prague           i300   c1   p5.5   index 0.05  loses ~17 clicks/10d  -
```

**Copy test**

```
Copy test: city-title-age-first (day 29 of 56, review 2026-11-05)
The age is the hook and the count is inventory, so naming the oldest tree before the number earns more clicks.

  control      n1    before 1.38  now 0.07  change -1.31

  TOO EARLY. At about 2 clicks per page per ten days this needs the full window; a lead now is noise wearing a result's clothes.
```

**Conclusion:** Search is the only channel with real data and it is still small: 1 clicks and 2 impressions on Google's freshest day (day before: c0/i47). Directional at best; no strategic conclusions from these volumes.

Search Console, the last 10 days Google will give us (its data lags 2-3 days, so the newest row is never yesterday).
```
SEO learning pass: 1 pages over 40 impressions, 1 of them clean
Index 1.00 means a page converts exactly as its position normally does.

SHARE OF TREES CARRYING A PHOTOGRAPH
   40% and over           n1   i230   c1    ctr  0.4%  index 0.07

   40% and over           n1   i230   c1    ctr  0.4%  index 0.07

   40% and over           n1   i230   c1    ctr  0.4%  index 0.07

   40% and over           n1   i230   c1    ctr  0.4%  index 0.07

SHARE WITH A SEASONAL PEAK
   40% and over           n1   i230   c1    ctr  0.4%  index 0.07

TREES ON THE PAGE
   over 25                n1   i230   c1    ctr  0.4%  index 0.07

AVERAGE STORY LENGTH
   170 and over           n1   i230   c1    ctr  0.4%  index 0.07

EARNED AND WASTED, worst first (real queries, converting under half)
These have the demand already. Fixing one is worth more than a new page.
   prague           i230   c1   p5.4   index 0.07  loses ~13 clicks/10d  -
```

**Copy test**

```
Copy test: city-title-age-first (day 28 of 56, review 2026-11-05)
The age is the hook and the count is inventory, so naming the oldest tree before the number earns more clicks.

  control      n1    before 1.38  now 0.07  change -1.31

  TOO EARLY. At about 2 clicks per page per ten days this needs the full window; a lead now is noise wearing a result's clothes.
```

- 3 of 5 produced no trees; 13 to 41 commands refused per run.

**What they made**

- Vienna +7, merge 10 written trees (Chicago, Fukuoka, Nuremberg, Portland, SF, Sao Paulo, Takachiho), de/ja overlays
- Dresden enrichment: city Naturdenkmal records, heights, two seasons
- Lucca enrichment: MASAF ids, garden and tower visiting facts
- Graz enrichment: register ids, Eggenberg park terms, two seasons
- Salzburg enrichment: Naturdenkmal register ids
- Padua enrichment: register ids, measurements, ticket facts
- Nuremberg enrichment: Bavarian Naturdenkmal ids
- Olympic National Park enrichment: NPS record for the Quinault spruce
- Belgrade enrichment: protected-tree register entries and measurements
- Bologna enrichment: RAMI register ids, Rizzoli cedar measurements
- Record when each page last changed
- Lyon enrichment: Pin de Bunge measurements from the city's release
- Luxembourg City enrichment: ANF register ids; log the run
- Leiden enrichment: Hortus opening hours and prices, two register links, one season

Machine: 5 chain attempts yesterday, 4 got real work time (~78 min total). Dead-in-seconds attempts cost nothing; few get-throughs means the usage window was full or closed.

**Conclusion:** Search is the only channel with real data and it is still small: 1 clicks and 41 impressions on Google's freshest day (day before: c4/i47). Directional at best; no strategic conclusions from these volumes.

**Where demand is going to waste**

Search Console, the last 10 days Google will give us (its data lags 2-3 days, so the newest row is never yesterday). The newest row is also still FILLING: Google backfills for days and backfills impressions harder than clicks, so it reads as an unusually good day and is not one (08-22 arrived as 12/769 and settled at 17/1030). **Copy test**

```
Copy test: city-title-age-first (day 27 of 56, review 2026-11-05)
The age is the hook and the count is inventory, so naming the oldest tree before the number earns more clicks.

  age_first    n12   before 0.77  now 0.86  change +0.09
  control      n10   before 0.75  now 0.45  change -0.30

  against control: +0.39 index points (promote at +0.25)

  TOO EARLY. At about 2 clicks per page per ten days this needs the full window; a lead now is noise wearing a result's clothes.
```

**What they made**

- Enrich New Orleans: Live Oak Society ids and girth; log
- Enrich Florence: AMI ids and measurements on 6 trees; log
- Enrich Sintra: ICNF entry and measurements on 2 trees; log
- Enrich Austin: register records, Old Baldy height and access; log

- Enrich Milan: MASAF ids and measurements; log

- Log: Glasgow enrichment run
- Enrich Glasgow: height on the Argyle Street Ash; register and measurements not closable
- Record when each page last changed
- Translate Ede to Dutch (7 trees); log
- Enrich Prague: register ids on 18 trees, girth on 1, season on 1; log

Machine: 6 chain attempts yesterday, 6 got real work time (~96 min total). Dead-in-seconds attempts cost nothing; few get-throughs means the usage window was full or closed.

**Conclusion:** Search is the only channel with real data and it is still small: 1 clicks and 14 impressions on Google's freshest day (day before: c4/i47). Directional at best; no strategic conclusions from these volumes.

## Weekly analysis 2026-10-06

Tenth weekly analysis. Compares 09-28 to 10-04 (the first week after the
demotion) with 09-21 to 09-27, read from the daily entries. 10-04 is still
partial in Search Console and the 10-05 daily entry is not in this file yet.

**Scorecard**

| Week | Impressions | Clicks | CTR | Avg position | Visits (beacon, 8d) | Directions (14d) | App-CTA (14d) | App downloads (first-time) | Waitlist total | Submissions total |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 09-21 to 09-27 | 15,217 | 377 | 2.5% | ~7.6 | about 1,440 (spike excluded) | 43 | 42 | 24+ (09-21 not in table) | 20 (Android) | 27 |
| 09-28 to 10-04 | 400 | 14 | 3.5% | 18 to 39 | 220 (09-27 to 10-04) | 34 | 22 | 13 | 20 (Android) | 32 |

**What moved:**
1. Search is at the floor. Impressions fell 97% (15,217 to 400) and clicks
   96% (377 to 14): about 55 impressions a day since 09-28 against about
   2,000 before. Average position went from 7.5 to 22 to 39, so Google shows
   the site far less everywhere, not a few pages losing. The 3.5% CTR is 14
   clicks and means nothing. No sign of recovery in 7 days; the change is
   the demotion, not a content trend.
2. Site visits followed: 220 beacon visits in 8 days, the daily number
   falling from 90 (09-27) to 0 to 30 (10-04 is partial). Pageviews now
   come mostly from our own app host (240 of 460), Google gives 100. Page
   load p50 3.8 s, p90 9.4 s.
3. The app held up better than the site. Downloads were 13 in the week
   (09-30 is missing from the table) against 24 or more the week before,
   with 09-26 (9) the peak. asc_downloads.py is moving again, so last
   week's top suggestion is closed. App events over 14 days: 144 opens, 515
   trees opened, 36 sightings from 68 installs. Volumes are small, and one
   install made 79 of a day's 109 events on 10-04.
4. Readers still give. 5 submissions this week (32 total), 3 accounts on
   10-01 and 2 on 10-05 (7 in 14 days), and six reader photographs went live
   (Reykjavik x3, Fukuoka, Melbourne, one added beside an existing lead).
   The ambassador asks on 10-05 (Reykjavik, Barcelona) are ours.
5. The machine is working: 529 minutes in its week so far, and on 10-06 it
   ran enrichment passes on Malaga, Paris, Dublin, Brussels, Edinburgh, New
   York, Munich, Tokyo, Rome and Palermo (register ids, girth, height).
   Right work for this phase; not measurable in search yet.

**Suggestions, ranked:**
1. **[run]** Keep the enrichment pass on the cities that held impressions
   before 09-28, in the digest's depth-roster order (prague, amsterdam,
   lisbon, seville, munich, berlin). 6,204 of 7,599 impressions in the
   10-day window sat on proven cities; nothing else in the data says which
   pages Google may restore first.
2. **[run]** Photographs on indexed pages with none come first. Only tree
   pages with a photograph are indexed since 10-06, 855 trees are kept out
   for lacking photo and pin, and reader photographs were the only recent
   additions to the indexable set. Work the sighting queue every knock.
3. **[sessie]** Check Search Console's Manual actions, Security issues and
   Page indexing reports, to confirm this is the algorithmic demotion we
   assume and not an action against the site. The data cannot separate the
   two, and the answer decides whether more content work or a
   reconsideration request is the next move.
4. **[run]** Do not read the zero-click pages as demand. /jauze,
   /wilparting and /komjatice show 84 to 222 impressions at 0 clicks, and
   their top queries ("kasukabe new city hall construction bid") are not
   tree searches. Keep them off the depth list unless seolearn's NOT_DEMAND
   rule clears them.
5. **[FOR HIDDE]** Decide how long recovery mode waits before a harder step
   (reconsideration request, or pruning more of the 8,935 noindexed pages).
   Search Console is flat at about 55 impressions a day for 7 days; the case
   for a second move builds if it is still flat after two more weeks.

**Watch next week:** daily impressions rising above 100 (first sign of
recovery), the Page indexing count, whether downloads hold near 2 a day
with search gone, and the share of pageviews coming from our own host.

**Copy test**

```
Copy test: city-title-age-first (day 25 of 56, review 2026-11-05)
The age is the hook and the count is inventory, so naming the oldest tree before the number earns more clicks.

  age_first    n15   before 0.79  now 0.83  change +0.04
  control      n10   before 0.75  now 0.41  change -0.34

  against control: +0.38 index points (promote at +0.25)

  TOO EARLY. At about 2 clicks per page per ten days this needs the full window; a lead now is noise wearing a result's clothes.
```

- Measuring since 2026-08-30, when Measure.swift went in. Unlinked to any account by design: an install id, the app version and the OS, nothing else.
- Ours is not in this table: 20 install(s), 472 events, the testing before the app went live on 2026-09-03. Any install first seen before that stays excluded, so testing on the same phone never reads as a stranger.
- 67 installs have ever sent anything. An install id is made fresh on each install, so this is an upper bound on people, not a count of them.
- Yesterday came from 6 installs, and the busiest made 16 of 6 events (267%). One install making most of a day is what our own reinstall looks like: add its id to excluded_installs in data/app-measure.json if it is ours.
- Tabs opened (14d): Discover 63; My trees 57; Map 46
- Trees recorded (14d): a tree we map 18; a tree we do not 14
  A sighting reaches our database only through an account. Signed out it stays on the phone, so this count can run ahead of the trees and photographs we actually receive.

**Where demand is going to waste**

Search Console, the last 10 days Google will give us (its data lags 2-3 days, so the newest row is never yesterday). The newest row is also still FILLING: Google backfills for days and backfills impressions harder than clicks, so it reads as an unusually good day and is not one (08-22 arrived as 12/769 and settled at 17/1030). Position is an average across every query, so it dips whenever we start ranking for something new:

- Content leads, biggest first. A lead is a query no page TITLE covers, so some are ranking problems on a page we have rather than a page to write ('vegas trees' against /las-vegas). Check before building:

**Copy test**

```
Copy test: city-title-age-first (day 24 of 56, review 2026-11-05)
The age is the hook and the count is inventory, so naming the oldest tree before the number earns more clicks.

  age_first    n18   before 0.78  now 0.75  change -0.02
  control      n14   before 0.66  now 0.50  change -0.17

  against control: +0.14 index points (promote at +0.25)

  TOO EARLY. At about 2 clicks per page per ten days this needs the full window; a lead now is noise wearing a result's clothes.
```

**Where demand is going to waste**

Search Console, the last 10 days Google will give us (its data lags 2-3 days, so the newest row is never yesterday). The newest row is also still FILLING: Google backfills for days and backfills impressions harder than clicks, so it reads as an unusually good day and is not one (08-22 arrived as 12/769 and settled at 17/1030). **Copy test**

```
Copy test: city-title-age-first (day 23 of 56, review 2026-11-05)
The age is the hook and the count is inventory, so naming the oldest tree before the number earns more clicks.

  age_first    n20   before 0.76  now 0.70  change -0.06
  control      n17   before 0.66  now 0.64  change -0.01

  against control: -0.05 index points (promote at +0.25)

  TOO EARLY. At about 2 clicks per page per ten days this needs the full window; a lead now is noise wearing a result's clothes.
```

- Measuring since 2026-08-30, when Measure.swift went in. Unlinked to any account by design: an install id, the app version and the OS, nothing else.
- Ours is not in this table: 20 install(s), 471 events, the testing before the app went live on 2026-09-03. Any install first seen before that stays excluded, so testing on the same phone never reads as a stranger.
- 65 installs have ever sent anything. An install id is made fresh on each install, so this is an upper bound on people, not a count of them.
- Yesterday came from 5 installs, and the busiest made 30 of 5 events (600%). One install making most of a day is what our own reinstall looks like: add its id to excluded_installs in data/app-measure.json if it is ours.
- Tabs opened (14d): Discover 60; My trees 56; Map 45
- Trees recorded (14d): a tree we do not 16; a tree we map 15
  A sighting reaches our database only through an account. Signed out it stays on the phone, so this count can run ahead of the trees and photographs we actually receive.

**Where demand is going to waste**

Search Console, the last 10 days Google will give us (its data lags 2-3 days, so the newest row is never yesterday). The newest row is also still FILLING: Google backfills for days and backfills impressions harder than clicks, so it reads as an unusually good day and is not one (08-22 arrived as 12/769 and settled at 17/1030). Position is an average across every query, so it dips whenever we start ranking for something new:

**Copy test**

```
Copy test: city-title-age-first (day 22 of 56, review 2026-11-05)
The age is the hook and the count is inventory, so naming the oldest tree before the number earns more clicks.

  age_first    n21   before 0.73  now 0.66  change -0.08
  control      n20   before 0.65  now 0.62  change -0.03

  against control: -0.05 index points (promote at +0.25)

  TOO EARLY. At about 2 clicks per page per ten days this needs the full window; a lead now is noise wearing a result's clothes.
```
